- **Role:** Resonance-Impedance Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Electrical Apparatus Short-Circuit Dynamic & Thermal Withstand

- **Role:** Deionized-Water Mechanic
- **Scenario:** In-situ Removal, Reassembly & Dynamic Tolerance Reset SOP for Rectifier Water-Cooling Chiller & Busbar Wear Components
- **Intro:** Deionized-Water Mechanic — In-situ Removal, Reassembly & Dynamic Tolerance Reset SOP for Rectifier Water-Cooling Chiller & Busbar Wear Components — Confirm work permit and plan LOTO isolation: Work zone restricted to 3 m from live busbars; authorized approach distance not less than 1.25 m without rated insulation.
- **Key tolerance:** Work zone restricted to 3 m from live busbars; authorized approach distance not less than 1.25 m without rated insulation.
- **Standards:** `No. 85`, `SANS 10142-1:2017`, `SANS 60947-1:2020`, `ISO 1101:2017`
